General Project-Information
A
1. CU-rail treatment to DIN 43673
2. Tightening torque the mechanical Screw-on connections from CU-rail according to DIN 43673
3. CU-rail are to mark with L1, L2, L3, N, PE (PEN)
4. Tightening torque of the connection bolt from resources according to the assemply instruction
B
5. All equipment label are placed on the Component and on the Mounting panel
6. At Ventilation of the Switchgear a Excess pressure ventilation is to use
7. Contact protection cover according to BGV A3 has to mount
8. Equipment specific gaskets has to mount
9. Resources and terminals those amid voltage at off position of the Main switch has to label
10. Terminals should be labeled on both sides (internal and external) with the corresponding number
C
11. Before mainswitch als conductions has to be wired in yellow till the last component.
12. After transport and installation all screw-on connections has been tighten up
13. All Mounting panels are equip with a extra mounting panels locator
14. All ontrol panels has to mark with the location designation from the outside
15. All enclosures are equip with a type label
16. It is not allowed to put promotion on the control panel
D
E
F
